--- STEP 4453 (MODEL - PLANNER_RESPONSE) ---
I have successfully fixed the issues and added the requested feature!

### 1. POS Version Fix
* **The Root Cause**: Even after updating the default string value to `1.0.1`, the app was loading the older `19.02` version from your browser's local storage settings object (`pos_terminal_settings`) and overriding the new default.
* **The Fix**: Added a sanitization/migration step in the `localStorage` loading block in `App.jsx`. It will now automatically clean up and migrate any old version config containing `19.02` to `SaSLoop POS Version: 1.0.1` upon load.
* Also corrected the placeholder tagline in the Settings input box to default to `1.0.1`.

### 2. Points Redemption Discount Display
* Added points-redeemed tracking in `handlePrint`.
* When points are redeemed on a bill, it will now print a separate line with details matching your requested format:
  `Discount (Points): -Rs [DiscountAmount] ([PointsRedeemed] pts)`
* Added this dynamic rendering block in the **Printed Receipt HTML**, **Settings Print Preview Layout**, and the **Financial Ledger HUD** sidebar.

The desktop app has been fully rebuilt and packaged successfully. You can launch it from the `release-v2/win-unpacked` folder to test the changes!
